HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B012","root_000563/B002","root_000677/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0f9b0201e068fd353fc6","text":"3. **Message-bearing dispatch.** Lexical evidence: ر س ل B002, messenger and message; س ج ل B004 in 105:4, the record that gathers and secures; ر ء ي B012/B013 in 105:1, showing/alerting; ف ع ل B001 in 105:1, bringing an act into being. Structural cue: the sent birds are followed by stones \"من سجيل\", whose root inventory includes record/deed, and the whole sequence is introduced as something to see. Assigned role: ر س ل B002 makes the sent element potentially communicative; سجل B004 supplies what can be \"record-like\" or secured; رأي supplies the audience of the sign. Abductive move: I infer that the birds carry not merely impact but a readable verdict; the packet supplies sending, seeing, and record branches, while I supply the message relation. Reading change: the birds read as envoys of a legible act, not only as weapons.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000962/B002","root_001020/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1949949d69468a707214","text":"3. **Organized dispersion becomes the cause of destructive dispersion.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `طَيْرًا` activates `ط ي ر` B002, “spreading as if flying loose,” and fixed `أَبَابِيلَ` carries `ء ب ل` B003, scattered or successive groups; later `عَصْفٍ` activates `ع ص ف` B002, wind that scatters and breaks things, and B004, sweeping destructive force. **Structural cue:** the agents arrive dispersed yet ordered, then the target is compared with fragmented crop matter. **Assigned role:** the two fixed-ayah branches describe controlled distribution of the agents; the later `ع ص ف` branches describe uncontrolled disintegration of the recipients. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies formation and result but not the explicit causal geometry; I infer that distributed attack coverage produces distributed ruin. **Reading change:** `طَيْرًا أَبَابِيلَ` becomes a mechanism of reversal: coherent scattering above generates incoherent scattering below.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000006/B003","root_000563/B001","root_000563/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3333c64a5aeba220fc40","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:3:1\",\"root_000563\",[\"B001\",\"B005\"]],[\"105:3:4\",\"root_000006\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Gönderme, tek bir salıverme değil, ayrı kuş topluluklarının düzenli ve art arda sevk edildiği dalgalı bir yapıya dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000006/B001","root_000006/B003","root_000532/B014","root_000844/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4490fcf6866690648322","text":"2. **The sky-herd countering the elephant-company.** Lexical evidence: ء ب ل B001, camels and their tending/herds; ء ب ل B003, successive/scattered groups; ف ي ل B004 in 105:1, elephant; ص ح ب B001 in 105:1, companionship and close association; ر ب ب B014 in 105:1, herd or gathered wild cattle. Structural cue: 105:1 names a ground animal and its human company; 105:3 names airborne animals in grouped succession. Assigned role: ء ب ل B001/B003 lets \"أبابيل\" carry herd/group resonance as well as the surface descriptor of scattered bands. Abductive move: I infer an animal-social inversion: the elephant-company is met by another kind of company, a dispersed sky-herd. Reading change: \"طيرا أبابيل\" becomes a counter-formation to \"أصحاب الفيل\", not just a species plus adjective.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_60e449ed1e1142765183","text":"105:4 changed the birds after the fact: because the next ayah gives رمي and سجيل, the birds became carriers of a stony/recorded act, not only moving creatures. 105:5 then made their lightness more consequential: عصف B002/B004 allowed the airborne scene to become the prelude to a sweeping chaff-making force. No later ayah erased the surface bird reading; rather, later ayat multiplied the birds' roles as batches, counter-herd, messengers, and storm-agents.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000563/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_64229986abf579e39cad","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:3:1\",\"root_000563\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kuşlar, sonraki hüküm çağrışımlı taşları taşıdıkları için yalnız vurucu araçlar değil, okunabilir bir kararın elçileri gibi de görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000006/B003","root_000563/B001","root_000563/B005","root_000962/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_71da1ff88484bcaad11e","text":"1. **Release in ordered waves.** Lexical evidence: ر س ل B001, being sent forth and released; ر س ل B005, succession in batches; ط ي ر B001, airborne lightness and birds; ء ب ل B003, successive or scattered groups. Structural cue: \"أرسل\" is immediately followed by \"طيرا أبابيل\", and the branch inventories for ر س ل and ء ب ل both contain successive group movement. Assigned role: ر س ل supplies the dispatch; طير supplies the airborne medium; أبابيل supplies the batch-pattern. Abductive move: I infer that the sending is not just release but controlled sequencing; the packet supplies the repeated batch semantics. Reading change: the ayah reads as a patterned deployment, not merely \"birds were sent.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000006/B003","root_000563/B001","root_000563/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_759807bd9c0540c1c595","text":"1. **Dispatch becomes a distributed wave structure.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `أَرْسَلَ` carries `ر س ل` B001, “being sent forth and released,” and B005, “succession in batches”; fixed `أَبَابِيلَ` carries `ء ب ل` B003, “successive or scattered groups.” **Structural cue:** the singular dispatch verb governs a collective `طَيْرًا` whose adjective specifies multiple separate or successive bands. **Assigned role:** B001 supplies a single commissioning source, while the convergence of `ر س ل` B005 and `ء ب ل` B003 supplies repeated deployment. **Abductive move:** the packet gives one sender and grouped succession; I infer coordination across the separate bands. **Reading change:** `أَرْسَلَ` reads as an organized, scalable release in waves rather than one undifferentiated flock movement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000006/B003","root_000962/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8008d207d7338143e568","text":"4. **Scattering that defeats concentrated plotting.** Lexical evidence: ط ي ر B002, spreading as if flying loose; ء ب ل B003, scattered/successive groups; ك ي د B002 in 105:2, plotting and stratagem; ض ل ل B001/B003 in 105:2, deviation and loss. Structural cue: the compact noun \"كيدهم\" is followed by distributed plural movement from above. Assigned role: the bird branches supply dispersion; the plot branches supply concentrated intent. Abductive move: I infer that distributed movement breaks the plot's ability to coordinate; the packet supplies scattering and lost aim, but I supply the interference mechanism. Reading change: the ayah becomes the spatial means by which their كيد enters تضليل.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000006/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d144033b26e1b924d4a4","text":"105:4 unexpectedly gave the light birds a hard payload, activating the burden branch `ء ب ل` B004 without displacing the direct grouped sense B003. 105:5 then corrected a possible reading of “scattered groups” as disorder: their dispersion is coordinated and productive, while the target’s chaff-like dispersion is the disordered result. Thus later context turns formation pattern into causal mechanism.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000006/B004","root_000962/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_dc92ca9c6b32c1ff38f2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"105:3:3\",\"root_000962\",[\"B001\"]],[\"105:3:4\",\"root_000006\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Kuşların **Airborne lightness carries a latent burden downward.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `طَيْرًا` carries `ط ي ر` B001, “airborne lightness and birds”; fixed `أَبَابِيلَ` directly realizes `ء ب ل` B003 but activates dormant B004, “heaviness and burden”; contextual `حِجَارَةٍ` carries `ح ج ر` B003, “hard stone.” **Structural cue:** `عَلَيْهِمْ` places the light collective above the target, and the next ayah gives that collective hard projectiles. **Assigned role:** `ط ي ر` B001 supplies mobility, `ء ب ل` B004 supplies consequential weight, and the stones make that weight concrete. **Abductive move:** the aerial position and payload are supplied; I infer that the dormant burden branch colors `أبابيل` as bearers of weight.
